# DICTIONARY

**OF THE**

# GALLA LANGUAGE

**COMPILED**

**BY**

## LAWRENCE TUTSCHEK. M. D.





**PART II.**

**MUNICH**  
**1845.**

&lt;!-- PDF Page 277: Blank Verso of Title Page --&gt;

&lt;!-- PDF Page 278 (Part II Preface / Editor&#39;s Note) --&gt;

The second part of the Dictionary of the Galla Language is intended as a companion to the first part and the Grammar which have been already laid before the public. The editor is conscious how much it lacks of being a complete vocabulary of this language; yet he believes that it will be useful to the student and serve as an aid to further investigations. Some discrepancies of orthography between it and the Grammar have been detected in the course of publication though they have been avoided with as much care as possible. It will devolve upon later inquirers to rectify these errors as well as to furnish a standard of correct pronunciation.

&lt;!-- PDF Page 279: Blank --&gt;

&lt;!-- PDF Page 280 (Part II Book Page 3) --&gt;
*(— 3 —)*

## A.

**Admit,** v. a. *azenziza*, 2. ppx. III. f. of *azena*, to enter.

**Admonish,** v. a. *gorza*. 1. px.

**Adopt,** v. a. *baldja*. 1. px. — 2. *dabalad'a*. 1. ppx. lit. I add to myself, I join to myself. — 3. *gubifad'a*. 1. ppx.; the latter word is used to signify adoption when one has a family already.

**Adopted son,** s. *baldja*. *n.* ox.

**Adoption,** s. *gada*. *n.* ox. from *gada*, to cede. There are two ways of adopting a child, one of which is called *gada guda*, great or complete adoption, and the other *gada t'ina*, the little or incomplete one.

**Adorn,** v. a. *zarara*. 1. ppx. lit. to draw lines. — to - one's self, *guyifad'a*. 1. ppx. from *guya*, splendour.

**Adulterer,** s. *zaño*. *n.* ox.

**Advance,** v. n. *yȧ* (*-aa*) 1. — 2. *ziga*. 1. px. (Am.) — to - with an army, *ziqi dula*.

**Adversary,** s. *amadyadyu*. *n.* ox. (Am. *amadjadji*. *n.*) — 2. *hamad'u*. *n.* ox.

**Adversity,** s. *d'ibe*. *n.* ox.

**Advice,** s. *mala* *n.* ox. — to give -, *mala*. 1. px.

**Affair,** s. *makari*. *n.* ox.

**Affect,** v. a. *zotchoza*. 2. px. III. f. of *zotchoa* (Am. *zadjoa*), to be agitated, affected.

**Affiance,** v. n. *hamarti kayad'a*. ox. ppx. lit. to put on a ring.

**Affirm,** v. a. *ee djed'a*. 1. px. lit. to say „yes“. — 2. *muga*. 1. px.

**Affix,** to - to each other, for ex. pearls, *waliti dira*. 1. px.

**Afflict,** v. a. *did'iba*. 1. ppx. Intens. of *d'iba*, it alarms. — to be -ed, *gadda*. 1. px.

**Afraid,** to be -, *nȧ* (*-aa*) 1. — 2. *rifad'a*. 1. ppx. — 3. *rogoma*. 1. ppx. — 4. *t'elã* (*-awa*). 2. — 5. *warera*. 1. px. (Am. *worera*.) — 6. *zodad'a*. 1. px. — to make -, *zodad'z'iza*. 2. px. III. f. of the foregoing verb.

**After,** pr. and adv. *boda*. px. — 2. *duba*. px. *ati na duba d'alad'te*, thou wast born after me. — 3. *duka*. px. *na duka ademi*, go behind me. — 4. *dyala*. px. — after to-morrow, *iftani*. px. from *ifa*, to be bright.

**After,** conj. *yomi* and *yomu*. px. (Ag. abbr. *yom* and *yo*; Am. *yenu*.)

**Afterwards,** adv. *gulana*. px.

**Again,** adv. is expressed by the preterit of the verb *debia*. 1. ppx. lit. to return, conjugated through all its persons with other verbs
*(Sig. 1\*)*

&lt;!-- PDF Page 281 (Part II Book Page 4) --&gt;
*(— 4 —)*

e. g. *ani debie zi wadjin adamoṅ d'acu*, I will not go again a hunting with thee.

**Against,** adv. *dyala*. px.

**Agate,** s. *baldji*. *n.* ox.

**Age,** of the same -, s. *hiria*. *n.* ox.

**Aged,** to be -, *bȧ* (*-aa*) 1. *ani barra afur baetani abanko narra due*, when I was four years old my father died.

**Aggrandize,** v. a. *gudifad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *guda*, to be tall.

**Aggress,** v. a. *halêla*. 1. px.

**Agitate,** v. a. *zotchoza*. 1. px. III. f. of *zotchoa*. 1. ppx. (Am. *zadjoa*), to be agitated; also *zotchofad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f.

**Ago,** not long -, adv. *zila*. px. (Am.)

**Agony,** s. *nafa*. nom. *nafni* or *nafti* px. — to be in -, *labã* (*-awa*) 2. and the Intens. *lalabã*. 2. — 2. *moga*. 1. px. — 3. *wirigad'a*. 1. ppx. (Am. *wurigad'a*).

**Agree,** v. n. *wal' djed'a*. 1. px. lit. to tell to each other. — to - on, or upon, *caba waliti cabad'a*. px. ppx. lit. to take the word (obligation) from each other. — to - with, *zifa*. 1. px. *zifa!* used as a vow: may it prosper!

**Agreeable,** to be -, *garoma*. 1. px. from *gari*, good. — 2. *miʿã* (*-awa*) 2. *o ati guda miʿofta*, O thou art so agreeable. — 3. *mararfama*. 1. ppx. passive of *mararza*, the III. f. of *marara*, to be beloved.

**Ah,** interj. to express amazement or pain, *ah!* — 2. *ufayi*. px. — 3. *wayo*. px.

**Air,** s. *qilenza*. *i.* px. (Am. *qile˜za*.) — in the open air, *adu keza*, lit. in the sun.

**Alarmed,** to be -, *rafama*. 1. ppx. the passive of the III. f. of *rá*, *raza*, to be in motion.

**All,** adj. *huntuma*. ppx. and *huntumtu*. px.

**Almost,** adv. is expressed by the verb *gȧ*. see about, adv.

**Alms,** to ask -, *wakad'a*. 1. px. (Am.) — to give -, *hirba*. 1. px.

**Alone,** adj. *koba*. ox. *kobako*, *kobaké*, *kobaza*, *kobaz'i*, *kobakeña*, *kobakezani*, *kobazani*; I, thou, he, she etc. alone. see Gram. n. 227. 5.

**Aloud,** adj. *ulfa*. px. lit. heavy. *zagale ulfeza*, to raise the voice, speak aloud.

**Also,** adv. *le*, adv. affix; *inile akana*, he likewise so.

&lt;!-- PDF Page 282 (Part II Book Page 5) --&gt;
*(— 5 —)*

**Altercate,** v. n. *d'ecama*. 1. ppx. with the dative. — 2. *falama*. 1. ppx. and *falma*. 1. px. (law-suit.) — 3. *morka*. 1. px. and *morkad'a*. 1. ppx.

**Alternately,** adv. *darba*, see part I. — 2. *wal* (*wali*) (Am. *wol*, *woli*.) recipr. pron. *wal gafa hodjana*, we work alternately.

**Although,** conj. *dėgi* and *dėgifiz*; *dėgi ini hamadele izaṅ adjezini*, although he did wrong, don't kill him! (Ag. *egi*) — 2. *oto* px. (Am. and Ag. *utu*.), followed by the adv. *le*; *oto ini dureza dyirule ini zorezamiti*, although he is rich, he is not a prince.

**Always,** adv. *ama-ama* or *amama*. px.

**Amass,** v. a. *tuteza*. 1. px. III. f. of *tuta*, to increase.

**Amazed,** to be -, *dyadya*. see wonder.

**Ambassador,** s. *labzi*. *n.* ox.

**Ambush,** s. *gadamzo*. *n.* ox. — 2. *kuta*. *n.* ox. or *kutan*. inv. ox. from *kuta*, to cut off. — 3. *milto*. *n.* ox. — 4. *teziza*. *n.* ox. *izan teziza tezizani*, they laid an ambush.

**Amiable,** to be -, *miʿã* (*awa*) 2.

**Amidst,** adv. *walkeza*. px. see Gram. n. 240.

**Amorous,** to be -, *bobã* (*-awa*) 2. — to make -, *bobeza*. 1. px. III. f. of the foregoing.

**Amusement,** s. *taba* *n.* ox.

**And,** part. *fi*, adv. affix. It is usually affixed, so that the vowel *i* is either assimilated with that of the following word, or is dropped altogether; *anif abanko*, I and my father; *obolonikeñafi laminkeña*, our brothers and cousins.

**Angel,** s. guardian angel, *ayana*. *i.* px. *ayaniko naṅ dyalad'u*, my angel loves me not, i. e. I do not succeed in my endeavours, I am in distress. — 2. *erga*. *n.* ox. from *erga*, to send.

**Anger,** s. *mut'a*. *n.* ox. — to talk with -, *watcha*, 1. px.

**Angry,** to be -, *ara*. 1. px. lit. to steam. — 2. *dalana*. 1. px. — 3. *gororia*. 1. ppx. — 4. *komad'a*. 1. ppx. constr. with the dat. case; *naṅ komad'in*, do not scold me. — 5. *watcha*. 1. px. — 6. *watchila*. 1. ppx. — to make -, *bobeza*. 1. px. III. f. of *bobã*. — 2. *buz'eza*. 2. px. III. f. of *buz'ã*, to be bitter. — 3. *d'ecamziza*. 2. px. III. f. of *d'ecama*. — 4. *dalanziza*. 2. px. III. f. of *dalana*.

**Animal,** s. *binenza*. *i.* px. (Am. *bine˜za*.)

**Ankle,** s. *mañe*. *n.* ox.

**Annex,** to - to each other, e. g. pearls, *waliti dira*. ppx. px. 1.

&lt;!-- PDF Page 283 (Part II Book Page 6) --&gt;
*(— 6 —)*

**Anoint,** v. a. *diba*. 1. px. — 2. *muda*. 1. px. — to - one's self, *dibad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. of *diba*. — 2. *mudad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. of *muda*. — 3. *guyifad'a*. — to cause to be oiled, *dibza*. 1. px. III. f. of *diba*.

**Ant,** s. *gonda*. *n.* ox. — anetermined species of ant: *mit'i*. prov. *bokeñi mitileṅ adjezu zi adjeza?* the rain kills not even a „*miti*“, will it kill thee?

**Antelope,** s. *kurube*. *n.* ox. — 2. *bozonu*. *n.* ox. (Ag.)

**Anxious,** to be -, *birʿefad'a*. 1. ppx. lit. to say „*brr*“. — 2. *zodad'a*. 1. px. — to make -, *zodad'z'iza*. 2. px. III. f. of *zodad'a*.

**Aperture,** s. (of vessels) *afan*. (*afani*) inv. ox. lit. mouth.

**Appear,** v. n. *mulada*. 1. ppx.

**Appearance,** to make it's -, *bafad'a*. 1. ppx. see, come up.

**Appetency,** s. *como* *n.* ox. from *come*, breast.

**Approach,** v. n. *d'iad'a*. 1. px. II. f. of the unused root *d'iã*. — 2. *gȧ* (*-aya*) 3. — 3. *yȧ* (*-aa*) 1.

**Appropriate,** v. a. *hambifad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *hamba*, to rest. — 2. *hirad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. of *hira*, to deal.

**Arbitrate,** v. a. *gadjara*. 1. ppx. (jur.)

**Arise,** v. n. *d'alad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. of *d'ala*, to bring forth.

**Arm,** upper-part of the -, *ire*. *n.* ox. — arm-full, s. *kazu*. *n.* ox. derived from the III. f. of *kã*, to rise, lit. as much as a man can take up.

**Armlet,** s. (of ivory) *ilbora*. *i.* px. (Am. *irbora*. Amh. IRBORA:) worn by men after they have killed an enemy. — 2. *gume*. *n.*

ox. — 3. *malla*. *n.* ox. — 4. *zomboco*. *n.* ox. of silver and worn from the wrist to the elbow.

**Ashamed,** to be -, *zalfad'a*. 1. ppx. from *zala*, a man's yard.

**Ashes,** s. *dara*. *n.* ox. — 2. *ramatchi*. *n.* ox. (Am.)

**Assail,** v. a. *ñad'a*. 1. px. inf. *ñad'z'u*. — 2. *oria*. 1. ppx.

**Assert,** v. a. *ee djed'a*. 1. px. lit. to say „yes“. — 2. *muga*. 1. px.

at once, adv. *irgataka*, from *irge*, place and *toko*, one. — 2. *aka tokoti*, from *aka*, like and *toko*.

Ag. — 3. *hoyu*. px. it is always placed after the sentence which it governs. *malif d'ufudidte ani zin djed'e hoyu*, why didst thou not come whereas I told it to thee. — 4. *kan*. **Bee-hive,** s. *komdo*. *n.* ox.

**Beer,** s. *farzo*. *n.* ox. a fermented beverage made from grain, especially wheat, a sort of beer which is the customary drink of the Gallas; entirely resembling the *ngaslo* of the *Yumale*. — 2. *dyalali*. *n.* ox. particular name for a *farzo* sufficiently fermented, from *dyalali*, clear, clean.

**Beetle,** s. (in general) *bombi*. *n.* ox.

**Before,** adv. *dura*. px. also a comparat. particle; *ani zi dura dyira*, I am before thee, I am older than thou; *ini durá*, (lit. he before) the first.

**Before,** conj. before that, *oto*. px. (Am. *utu*) followed by the negation; *oto ini bozona hillit'ini iza cabani*, before he reached the forest they caught him.

**Beg,** v. a. *kad'ad'a*. 1. ppx. inf. *kad'ad'z'u*. ox. always constr. with the postpos. *ni* (*n*). — to -, v. n. *abakad'a*. 1. ppx. (Ak. to tattle.)

**Beggar,** s. *kad'ad'u*. *n.* ox. from *kad'ad'a*, to pray, beg.

**Begin,** v. a. *caba*. 1. px. lit. to seize; espec. *dyal-caba*, lit. to seize beneath. — 2. *kȧ* (*-aa*). 1. constr. with the postp. *ti*; *ini hodjad'z'uti kae*, lit. he rose to the work, i. e. he began to work. Also the verb *baria*. 1. ppx. which expresses the beginning of any action; *lafti barienan godane*, lit. when the earth awoke (?) he set off, i. e. when it dawned; *bule barienan*, when evening began; *birran barie*, spring has begun.

**Beginning,** s. *mata*. *n.* ox. *ini mata dubi gade*, he lost the (head) beginning of his speech.

**Boast,** v. n. *d'ad'ad'a*. 1. ppx. Intens. of the II. f. of *d'ã*, to beat. — 2. *dyadyaba*. 1. ppx. Intens. of *dyaba*, to be strong.

**Boat,** s. *bidiru*. *n.* ox.

**Body,** s. *d'agna*. *i.* px. — dead -, *rêfa* *i.* px.

**Bog,** s. *rare*. *n.* ox. — 2. *tchafe*, *n.* ox.

**Boil,** v. a. and n. *afêla*. 1. px. — 2. *dafca*. 1. px. — 3. *damfa*. 1. px. — 4. *koka*. 1. px. — 5. *toltcha*. 1. px. — to - for one's self, *tolfad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *tola*, to be good. — to cause to -, *afêlziza*. 2. ppx. III. f. and *afêlzifad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *afêla*.

**Bold,** adj. *dyabeza*. px. from *dyaba*, strong.

**Bolter,** s. *zulitsha*. *n.* ox.

**Bone,** s. *lafe*. *n.* ox. — back-bone, *moo*. *n.* ox.

**Boop,** s. (bird) *karole*. *n.* ox.

**Booty,** to make -, *danaba*. 1. ppx.

**Border,** s. *care*. *n.* ox. from *cara*, top, edge. — 2. *randa*. *i.* px. — - of a river, *gama*. *n.* ox.

**Bore,** v. a. *cota*. 1. px. and the II. f. *cotad'a*. 1. ppx. — 2. *mut'a*. 1. px.

**Borer,** s. *mut'a*. *n.* ox.

**Born,** to be -, *d'alad'a*. 1. ppx. — new-born, adj. *d'alad'u*. ox.

**Borrow,** v. a. *dabarfad'a*. 1. ppx. lit. I let or make something go over to myself, IV. f. of *darba*, to pass by. — 2. *ergifad'a*. 1. ppx. lit. to cause to send anything for one's self.

**Bosom,** s. (the female) *harma*. *i.* px.

**Botch,** v. a. *zuba*. 1. px.

**Both,** adj. *latshanu*. ox.

**Bottom,** s. (of vessels, utensils) *fagara*. *i.* px. — 2. *hudu*. *n.* ox. — to go to the -, *lit'a*. 1. px.

**Bounce,** v. n. *dadada-god'a*. 1. px. nat. sound, lit. to make „*dadada*“.

**Bound,** v. a. (in the mortar), *cat'acat'a*. 1. px.

**Boundary,** s. *dari*. *n.* ox. — 2. *mata*. *n.* ox. — to be or dwell on the -, *moga*. 1. px.

**Boundary-stone,** s. *dari*. *n.* ox.

**Bow,** v. a. *djalliza*. 2. ppx. III. f. of *djalla*, to be curved. — v. n. *gadi djed'a*. 1. px. — 2. *agama*. 1. ppx. espec. to - on the side.

**Boy,** s. *gurba*. *n.* ox. till the age of eighteen years.

**Bracelet,** s. there are many kinds of bracelets, viz. *girdja*. *n.* px. a thin -, worn on the upper-arm. — *gudidja*. *n.* px. a great -, military decoration. — *ilka*. *n.* ox. - of ivory, from *ilkan*, tooth. — *malla*. *n.* ox. etc.

**Braid,** v. a. *hulugza*. 1. ppx. and espec. *hulugziza*. 2. px. III. and V. f. of *huluga*, to creep through a thing. — 2. *gonogona*. 1. (⏑ — ⏑ ⏑)

**Brain-pan,** s. *watchidi*. *n.* ox. (Am. *wotchidi*.)

**Brains,** s. *zamu*. *n.* ox. (Am. *d'uka mata*, lit. marrow of the head.)

**Bran,** s. *hincatchi*. *n.* ox.

**Branch,** s. *d'ame*. *n.* ox. — 2. *d'ancu*. *n.* px. greater than *d'ame*, (the first arm from the trunc upwards.)

**Brave,** adj. *djagna*. px. — 2. *god'a*. px. — to be -, *djagna*. 1. px.

**Braving,** adj. *dyabeza*. px.

**Bray,** v. n. (of an ass) *alaga*. 1. ppx. — 2. *alalaza*. 2. px.

**Bread,** s. in general, *budena*. *i.* ox. — a thick loaf, *gaguba*. *n.* ox. — white unleavened bread like the easter-cake of the Israelites, *mat'ino*. *n.* ox. — a particular kind of small loaves, *bitile*. *n.* ox. — to make -, *bitila*. 1. ppx. and *bitilad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f.

**Break,** v. a. *tchabza*. 1. px. III. f., and *tchabzad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *tchaba*. — 2. *bakakza*. 1. px. III. f. of *bakaka*. — 3. *hurreza*. 1. px. III. f. of *hurra*. — to - open (of thieves), *cota*. 1. px. prop. to dig. — to - up, *gamdal-god'a*. 1. px. nat. sound. — to - a promise, *hafa*. 1. px. — to - in little pieces, *fadafad'a*. 1. ppx. (Am. *fatfatad'a*. 1. ppx.) — to - in, *tchabza*. 1. px. c. dativ. *hatun manati tchabze*, a thief has broken into the house. — to - in (of horses), *gêdjibza*. 1. ppx. III. f. of *gêdjiba*, to be broken in.

**Break,** v. n. *tchaba*. 1. px. — 2. *bakaka*. 1. ppx. — 3. *boruka*. 1. ppx. — to - in pieces, *tchaba*. 1. px.

**Breast,** s. (of human beings and beasts) *come*. *n.* ox. — the female -, *harma*. *i.* px. — breast-bone, *handarafa*. *ni.* px.

**Breath,** s. *hafura*. *i.* px.

**Breathe,** v. n. *hargana*. 1. ppx. — 2. *hurza*. 1. px. and *huriza*. 2. px. — to - at, *hafura bafad'a*. 1. ppx. constr. with *ti*. lit. to make go out the breath.

**Breech,** s. *hudu*. *n.* ox. — 2. *fagara*. *i.* px.

**Breeches,** s. *zure* *n.* ox. — -, made of leather, *d'aku* *n.* ox. — -, made of a cloth which is wound about the limbs, *marto*. *n.* ox.

**Bride,** s. *herumti*. *n.* ox.

**Bridegroom,** s. *fud'a*. *n.* ox.

**Bride-man,** s. *mari*. *n.* ox.

**Bridge,** s. *buza*. *n.* ox. — 2. *rigidja*. *i.* ox. — a little -, *nora*. *i.* px.

**Bright,** to be -, *ifa*. 1. px. and *ifad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. — to make -, *ibza*. 1. px. III. f.

**Brighten,** v. n. *dyalalaga*. 1. px.

**Brightness,** s. *balagi*. *n.* ox. — 2. *guya*. *i.* px.

**Brim,** s. *care*. *n.* ox. from *cara*, top, edge. — 2. *randa*. *i.* px.

**Bring,** v. a. *fida*. 1. px. usually connected with *d'ufa*, to come. *ini fide d'ufe*, lit. he brought came, i. e. he brought. — to - about, *fullaza*. 2. px. III. f. of *fullã*, to get through. — to - back, *debiza*. 2. ppx. III. f. of *debia*, to return. — to - forth, *d'ala*. 1. px. and *dȧ* (*-aya*) 3. see to bear. — to - forwards, *hicza*. 1. px. III. f. of *hica*, to go away. — to - home, *galtsha*. 1. px. III. f. of *gala*, to go home. — to - near, *geza*. 1. px. III. f. of *gȧ*, to approach. — to - out, *bafad'a*. 1. px. IV. f. of *bȧ*, to go out. — to - together, *kuza*. 2. px. and *kufad'a*. 1. ppx. III. and IV. f. of a root *kua* not in use. *kanan ani garakotin kufad'e qit'uma zi hima*, lit. what I in my heart gathered (i. e. thought), all tell I to thee. — to - up, *ad'aba*. 1. ppx. probably from *d'aba*, to put; and *gudiza*. 2. px. from *guda*, to be tall.

**Brink,** s. *care*. *n.* ox. from *cara*, top, edge. — 2. *randa*. *i.* px.

**Broach,** v. a. *dururfad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *durura*, to well, spring. — 2. *dyigza*. 1. px. III. f. of *dyiga*, to well.

**Broad,** adj. *balʿa*. px. — to be -, *balʿa*. 1. px. and espec. *balʿad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. — to make -, *balʿeza*. 1. px. III. f. and *fageza*. 1. px. from *fago*, far, distant.

**Brood,** v. a. *amad'a*. 1. ppx. (Am. *hamad'a*. 1. ppx.)

**Brook,** s. *dololo*. *n.* ox.

**Broom,** s. *corogonda*. *n.* ox.

**Broth,** s. *maraqi*. *n.* s. ox.

**Brother,** s. *oboleza*. *i.* px. Vocat. abbrev. *ya ob* instead of *ya obolezako*, O my brother; plur. *oboloni* and *obolá*. px. — german -, *had'o*. *n.* ox. — brother-in-law, *zoda*. *n.* ox.

**Brown,** adj. *magala*. px. — to be -, *magalã* (*-awa*) 2. — 2. *arawa*. 2. px.

**Browse,** v. a. (of birds and cattle), *mura*. 1. px.

**Bruise,** v. a. *bakakza*. 1. px. III. f. of *bakaka*, to break. — 2. *borukza*. 1. px. III. f. of *boruka*, to break. — 3. *docoza*. 2. px. — 4. *t'uca*. 1. px. (Am. *tuca*.)

**Brush,** s. *hafarza*. *n.* ox.

**Brush,** v. a. *haga*. 1. px.

**Buffalo,** s. *gafarza*. *i.* px. — 2. *korma*. *i.* px.

**Buffoon,** s. *radji*. *n.* ox. (Am.)

**Bug,** s. *gegio*. *n.* ox.

**Build,** v. a. *idyara*. 1. px. and *idyarad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. — to - together, *wal caba*.

**Building,** s. *idyari*. *n.* ox. — the act of - a house, *dyadani*. *n.* ox. *dyadaninke atami dyira?* how does your house-building proceed?

**Bull,** s. *korma*. *i.* px. *korma bordeda*, a bull with an erect hump, *korma gurra* (ear), a bull with a hanging hump. — 2. *ulfin*. inv. ox.

**Bumbailiff,** s. *cabi*. *n.* ox. from *caba*, to seize, etc.

**Bunch,** s. *hafa*. *ni.* px.

**Bundle,** to make a -, *gumeza*. 2. px. lit. to make round, III. f. of *guma*, to be round.

**Burn,** v. n. *bobã* (*-awa*) 2. — 2. *guba*. 1. px. and *gubad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. and *guguba*. 1. ppx. Intens. of *guba*. — 3. *owʿa*. 1. px. — 4. *lala*. 1. px. trans. and *lalad'a*. 1. ppx. espec. of urtica and similar things.

**Burst,** v. n. *hurrã* (*-awa*) 2. — 2. *d'oa*. 1. px. — 3. *bakaka*. 1. ppx. to - in pieces, trans. *bakakza*. 1. px. III. f. of *bakaka*.

**Bury,** v. a. *awala*. 1. px. (Am. *owala*.)

**Bushes,** s. *bozona*. *i.* px.

**Business,** s. *makari*. *n.* ox. — to be a man of -, *makara*. 1. ppx.

**Bustle,** s. *huru*. *n.* ox.

**Busy,** to be -, *arifad'a*. 1. ppx. lit. to run. — 2. *d'amã* (*-awa*) 2. — 3. *d'id'ad'a* and the Intens. *did'id'ad'a*. 1. ppx. — 4. *dyardyara*. 1. ppx.

**But,** adv. and conj. *male*. ox. a word which in all its changes entirely answers to the English „but“. *mali gonare inigalla male?*
*(Sig. 2)*

&lt;!-- PDF Page 295 (Part II Book Page 18) --&gt;
*(— 18 —)*

what should we begin but go home? *lafa kaetaṅ bou, wal' na d'ibe male*, I weep not in vain but something pains me. — 2. *eda*. (*ed'a*.) px. *eda dargagon kuni iza adjezu fed'eti manati d'okad'ani*, but these young man who would kill him concealed themselves in the house.

**Butcher,** s. *cunt'itcha*. *i.* px.

**Butcher,** v. a. *cala*. 1. px. — II. f. *calad'a*. 1. ppx. to - for one's self.

**Butter,** s. *d'ad'a*. *n.* ox. Prov. *d'ad'an ibidti bue hindebiu*, butter fallen into the fire does not come back again. — to make -, *raza*. 2. px. properly to shake, to move to and fro.

**Butter-milk,** s. *bad'u*. *n.* ox. lit. support, basis, scl. of the butter.

**Butterfly,** s. *bilatsha*. *i.* px.

**Button,** s. (on the spear) *djimfu*. *n.* ox.

**Buy,** v. a. *bita*. 1. px. — to - for one's self, *bitad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f.

**By,** pr. and adv. *bira*. postp. px. — 2. *dyala*. postp. px.

## C.

**Cabinet,** s. *gararad'a*. *ni.* px.

**Cackle,** v. n. (of a hen) *kakiza*. 2. ppx.

**Cajole,** v. a. *egad'a*. 1. ppx. lit. to wag with the tail.

**Calf,** s. *djibitcha*. *i.* ox. — 2. *gua*. *n.* ox. — 3. *tshabi*. *n.* ox.

**Calf,** s. (of the leg) *zarba*. *n.* ox. — 2. *fon dyabo*, lit. thick flesh.

**Call,** v. a. *wama*. 1. px. II. f. *wamad'a*. 1. ppx. to - for one's self, for assistance. — 2. *djed'a*. 1. px. — 3. *maca d'ã*. 2. lit. to beat the name.

**Calumniate,** v. a. *dudubad'a*. 1. px. II. f. Intens. of a root *duba* not in use; *dubiza*. 2. ppx. and the Intens. *dudubiza*. 2. ppx. III. f. — 2. *hamad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. of *hama*, to be bad. — 3. *lafafa*. 1. px. — 4. *maca fud'a*. 1. px. lit. to take the name.

**Calumniator,** s. *amadyadyu*. *n.* ox. (Am. *amadjadji*. *n.* ox.) — 2. *hamad'u*. *n.* ox. — 3. *lafaftu*. *n.* ox. and *lafaftudatu*. *n.* px. — 4. *kaz'akaz'tu*. *n.* ox.

**Camel,** s. *gimala*. *i.* px. plur. *gimalu*. (Am. *camale*. *n.* ox.)

**Can,** v. n. *dandȧ*. (*-aya*) 3. px. *ani dandȧ hind'ufu, ati dandeze hind'uftu, ini dandȧ hind'ufu*. etc. lit. I can I come not, thou

&lt;!-- PDF Page 296 (Part II Book Page 19) --&gt;
*(— 19 —)*

canst thou comest not, i. e. I cannot come, etc. — I cannot, *dad'aba*. 1. px. Intens. of the II. f. of *d'aba*, to want.

**Cancer,** s. *barari*. *n.* ox.

**Candid,** adj. *fanqe*. ox.

**Candle,** s. *gibbo* or *gipo*. *n.* ox. — 2. *tchezo*. *n.* ox. — 3. *ibza*. *n.* ox.

**Cane,** s. *dagale*. *n.* ox.

**Cannibal,** s. *bulgu*. *n.* ox. man-eater, a ghost who is often mentioned in the tales of the Gallas. — *Bauda*, a female cannibal, half man half animal; the word is perhaps related to *buda*, evil eye.

**Capricious,** adj. *dawe*. ox.

**Capture,** v. a. *caba*. 1. px. and II. f. *cabad'a*. 1. ppx. — to cause to be -d, *cabziza*. 2. px. and *cabzifad'a*. 1. ppx. III. and IV. f. of *caba*.

**Caravan,** s. *imaltu*. *n.* px.

**Care,** to take -, *ilalad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. of *ilala*, to see. — to have - of, *ega*. 1. px. lit. to wait.

**Careful,** to be -, *cobã*. (*-awa*) 2. ox. and Intens. *cocobã*.

**Caress,** v. a. *gabara*. 1. ppx. and Intens. *gagabara*. — 2. *amad'a*. 1. ppx. — 3. *waz'awaz'a*. 1. (⏑ — ⏑ ⏑) — 4. *zozoba*. 1. ppx.

**Carpet,** s. *erbe*. *n.* ox.

**Carry,** v. a. *bad'a*. 1. px. — 2. *gura*. 1. px. — to - away, *gura* and *gurad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. with the middle sense. — to - through, *tcheziza*. 2. ppx. III. f. of *tchea*, to penetrate. — to - too far, *owʿiza*. 2. ppx. and *owʿifad'a*. 1. ppx. III. and IV. f. of *owʿa*, to be hot.

**Carve,** v. a. *cota*. 1. px. — 2. *djadjia*. 1. ppx. (Am.) — 3. *kuta*. 1. px. *kutad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. — 4. *mura*. 1. px. — to - food, *buza*. 2. px. III. f. of *buȧ*, to come down.

**Carving-instrument,** s. *cofora*. *n.* ox.

**Cascade,** s. *findja*. *n.* ox. see, cataract.

**Cast,** v. a. *ȧ*. (contr. from *aa*) 1. *ibida manakeza ae*, he cast fire into the house. — 2. *darba*. 1. (*-ita*) px. — 3. *naca*. 1. px. *d'ad'en goradiza iti naqe*, the porcupine cast its stings against him. — to - away, *gada*. 1. px.

**Castigate,** v. a. *d'ana*. 1. px. usually connected with *yaka*, punishment. *Wacayo yaka zi d'anufdyira*, God will punish thee; III. f. *d'aniza*, to have castigated. — 2. *ad'aba*. 1. ppx. probably from *d'aba*, to put. — 3. *zerad'a*. 1. ppx. — 4. *yaka d'ã*. (*-awa*) 2. lit. to beat punishment.

**Castle,** the royal -, s. *mazara*. *n.* ox.
*(Sig. 2\*)*

&lt;!-- PDF Page 297 (Part II Book Page 20) --&gt;
*(— 20 —)*

**Castrate,** v. a. *kollaza*. 2. px. the root *kollã* has not been discovered.

**Casuar,** s. Ornithol. *tumza*. *i.* px.

**Cat,** s. *adalla*. *i.* px. — 2. *ature*. *n.* ox. (Am. *adure*.) — wild -, *wongo*. *n.* ox.

**Cataract,** s. *findja*. *n.* ox. There is a certain cataract in *Hambᵒ* down which criminals are thrown, called *findja Lali*, *Lali* being the name of the delinquent who was first plunged into it. — 2. *gotu*. *n.* ox. greater than *findja*. (Am.)

**Catch,** v. a. *caba*. 1. px. and II. f. *cabad'a*. 1. ppx. — to cause to be caught, *cabziza*. 2. px. III. f. and *cabzifad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f.

**Caterpillar,** s. *djirango*. *n.* ox.

**Cattle,** s. in general, *lon* (*loni*) inv. plur. tant. masc. The following are some of the peculiar expressions for the different ages and singularities of cattle: *tshabi*. *n.* ox. calf in the age when the horns break forth. — *djibitcha*. *i.* ox. calf, a little older. — *wofana*. — *godiyo*. *n.* ox. pl. *godiyoni*, gelded ox, used for labouring. — *korma*, *i.* px. buffalo which see. — *ulfin*. inv. ox. bull. (?). — *zanga*. *n.* ox. fattened ox. — *rad'a*. *i.* px. a cow two years old. — *goromza*. *i.* ox. a heifer old enough to breed. — *zã*. (contr. from *zawa*) nom. *zani*. cow in general. — *dulatcha*. *i.* ppx. a cow come to full growth, etc.

**Cautious,** adj. *cobe*. ox. from *cobã*, to attend to. — to be -, *djila*. 1. px. — 2. *ilalad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. of *ilala*, to see.

**Cavity,** s. *bola*. *i.* px.

**Cease,** v. n. *darba*. 1. px. (2. pers. *dabarta*. see Gram. num. 131.) — 2. *d'uma*. 1. px. — 3. *rawad'a*. 1. ppx. — 4. *tshama*. 1. px. *bokeñi tshame*, *d'ume*, the rain ceased.

**Cedar,** s. Bot. *gadra*. *n.* ox. (Am. *gadira*.)

**Cede,** v. a. *gada*. 1. px. *mé mutsha kana na gadi*, pray cede (or give) this child to me.

**Ceiling,** s. *keñan*. inv. ox. related either to *kã*, to lay, keep; or to *keza*, postp. in.

**Celebrate,** v. a. *ayanfad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *ayana*, to be holy.

**Ceremonies,** to make -, *gabara*. 1. ppx. and the Intens. *gagabara*. 1. ppx.

**Ceremonious,** to be -, *zodad'a*. 1. px.

**Certainly,** adv. *o*, affix. *nuo homaṅ gone*, *maf nu danta?* we have certainly done nothing (no harm), why dost thou punish us?

**Chaff,** s. *habagi*. *n.* ox. (Am. *mad'o*. *n.* ox.) — 2. *tchiti*. *n.* ox. (Am. *tchidi*.)

&lt;!-- PDF Page 298 (Part II Book Page 21) --&gt;
*(— 21 —)*

**Chain,** s. *mari*. *n.* ox.

**Chair,** s. *bardyuma*. *ni.* px. (Am. *mardyuma*. Ag. *hardyuma*.) — a little -, *tupilo*. *n.* ox.

**Chamber,** s. *haga*. *ni.* px. opposite to *gola* (room) and *d'inka* (bed-chamber). — chamber-maid, *haganufi* (*hagan ufi*) either derived from *haga*. 1. px. to brush, sweep, or from *haga* partition, chamber.

**Chameleon,** s. *garara*. *n.* ox. Prov. *ini gararati bifuza didirad'a*, he changes his colour like a chameleon.

**Champion,** a man whose exploits give him the right to anoint himself, *gondala*. *i.* px.

**Change,** v. a. *didira*. 1. px. — in the sense: to act alternately, *darba*. 1. (2. pers. *dabarta*, with transposed consonants, see Gram. num. 131.) px. *izan garᵃ garᵃ darbaniti hodjad'u*, they work alternately; *darba* means properly: to cease, and the idea of „change“ is explicable in this phrase: one ceases when the other begins.

**Charge,** v. a. *halêla*. 1. px. — to - a gun, *horfa*. 1. px.

**Chase,** s. *adamo*. *n.* ox.

**Chase,** v. a. *adamza*. 1. px. — to - away, *baza*. 2. px. III. f. of *bȧ*, to go out. — 2. *aria*. 1. ppx. — 3. *dabarza*. 1. px. III. f. of *darba*, to pass by. — 4. *d'id'imza*. 1. px. the root *d'id'ima* or *d'ima* has not been found.

**Chattering,** s. *dubi djamad'a*. *djamad'a*. 1. ppx. is the II. f. of *djama*, to be blind, (*dubi*, word.)

**Cheap,** adj. *lafa*. px. — to be -, *lafa*. 1. px. — 2. *buz'ã*. (*-awa*) 2. (Am. *buçã*.) — to make -, *buz'eza*. 2. px. III. f. of *buz'ã*.

**Cheek,** s. *madi*. *n.* ox.

**Cheek-tooth,** s. *ao*. *n.* ox. The four grinders which stand near the eye-teeth are called: *moge ao*, lit. the exterior grinders.

**Cheer,** v. a. *gamad'z'iza*. 2. px. III. f. of *gamad'a*, to be glad.

**Cheese,** s. *ititu*. *n.* ox. from *itã*, to swell. — 2. *tshubad'a*. *n.* ox. from *tshuba*, to plunge.

**Cherish,** v. a. *amad'a*. 1. ppx. (Am. *hamad'a*.) — 2. *gabara*. 1. ppx. and Intens. *gagabara*. 1. ppx. — to be -ed, *mararfama*. 1. ppx. prop. the passive of *mararza*, III. f. of *marara*, to be dear.

**Chew,** v. n. *lucuma*. 1. ppx.

**Chicken,** s. *djudji*. *n.* ox. — 2. *çôko*. *n.* ox.

**Chide,** v. n. *duta*. 1. px. — 2. *watchila*. 1. ppx.

&lt;!-- PDF Page 299 (Part II Book Page 22) --&gt;
*(— 22 —)*

**Chief,** s. *bai*. *n.* ox. from *bȧ*, to go out.

**Child,** s. (in general) *ilma*. *i.* px. plur. *ilman*. — 2. *d'ala*. *n.* ox. and *d'alo*. *n.* ox. from *d'ala* to give birth to; *d'alakoti na toli*, bless my children! (in prayers.) — 3. *mutshole*. *n.* ox. Collect. — new-born -, and as long it is suckled, *mutsha*. *n.* ox. — - in the age before it begins to walk, *daima*. *n.* ox. from *daima*, to creep on all fours. — - till the age of fourteen years, *hidjole*. *n.* ox. — the youngest - in the family, *gudizu*. *n.* ox. *ani gudizun d'alad'e*, I was the last born, from *gudiza*, to educate.

**Childhood,** during the -, adv. *duri*. px. (Am. *duru*.) *duratᵃ*, *durati*. ppx. from *dura*, before, earlier.

**Chilly,** to be -, *d'amod'a*. 1. ppx. *ani guda d'amod'era*, I feel very cold.

**Chin,** s. *areta*. *i.* px.

**Chine,** s. *harira*. *i.* px. — 2. *moo*. *n.* ox.

**Chink** between the two upper fore-teeth which is filed larger in order to spit through (singular custom of the Gallas), *karu*. *n.* ox. (Am. *karʿu*.)

**Chip,** s. *huba*. *ni.* px.

**Chirp,** v. n. (of birds) *watcha*. 1. px.

**Chisel,** s. *giridi*. *n.* ox.

**Choir-leader,** s. *baftu*. ox. fem. usually with *wetu*, *wetu baftu*, lit. a maiden who makes songs go out (scl. of her mouth), from *baza*, to make go out.

**Choke,** v. a. *ukamza*. 1. px. III. f. of *ukama*, to close the mouth. — to be -d, *hud'ama*. 1. ppx. passive of *hud'a*, to strangle.

**Choose out,** v. a. *cocobã*. (*-awa*) 2. ox. Intens. of *cobã*, to be attentive. — 2. *foa*. 1. px. and II. f. *foad'a*. 1. ppx. to - for one's self.

**Chop off,** v. a. *cua*. 1. px. (Am. *cuʿa*.)

**Churn,** s. *roo*. *n.* ox. from *reeʿ*, goat, because churns are made of goat's-leather.

**Cicatrice,** s. *d'ana*. *n.* ox. (Am. *godaniza*. *fni.* px.)

**Circulate,** v. n. *marza*. 1. px. from *mara*, to turn.

**Circumcision,** s. *djara*. *n.* ox. properly the feast where the surgical operation is performed.

**Circumspect,** adj. *cobe* ox. from *cobã*, to be attentive.

**Claim,** s. *cabdo*. *n.* ox. from *caba*, to seize, hold. *ini cabdo nara caba*, he has a claim on me. — 2. *liqi*. *n.* ox.

**Clammy,** to be or become -, *d'ogonã*. (*-awa*). 2.

&lt;!-- PDF Page 300 (Part II Book Page 23) --&gt;
*(— 23 —)*

**Clandestine,** adj. *cat'iza*. ppx. *cat'iza ademe*, he walked secretly, lightly.

**Clap,** v. n. *cab-djed'a*, nat. sound, lit. to say „*cab*“. — 2. *tub-djed'a*, also a nat. sound.

**Clasp,** s. *dagale*. *n.* ox.

**Claw,** s. *kote*. *n.* ox.

**Clay,** s. *gumbi*. *n.* ox. — 2. *zube*. *n.* ox. — a sort of - which is said to be eaten by men and cattle, *hȧ*.

**Clean,** adj. *dyalali*. ox. — 2. *culculu*. ox. (*cuculu*?) — to be -, *cula*. (*culã*?)

**Clean,** v. a. *culeza*. 2. px. and *culefad'a*. 1. ppx. III. and IV. f. of *cula*, to be clean. — 2. *zoca*. 1. px.

**Cleanse,** v. a. *hara*. 1. px. — 2. *hat'ã*. (*-awa*) 2. — 3. *zoca*. 1. px.

**Clear,** adj. *culculu*. ox. (*cuculu*?) — 2. *dyalali*. ox. *farzon kuni dyalalid'a*, this beer is clear. — to be -, *ifa*. 1. px. and II. f. *ifad'a*. 1. ppx. — to make -, *ibza*. 1. px. III. f.

**Clear,** v. a. (a room) *djarafa*. 1. ppx. — to - the throat, the voice, *cufȧ*. (*-aa*) 1. ox. and *akfad'a*. 1. ppx. nat. sound, lit. to make „*ak*“. — to - off (of fluids), intr. *dyalalaga*. 1. px.

**Cleave,** v. a. *falada*. 1. ppx.

**Cleaving,** to be or become -, *d'ogonã*. (*-awa*) 2.

**Clement,** to be -, *tola*. 1. px.

**Clemency,** s. *t'uri*. *n.* ox. which see in the I. part.

**Clingy,** to be or become -, *d'ogonã*. (*-awa*) 2.

**Clink,** v. n. *till-djed'a*. 1. px. nat. sound, lit. to say „*till*“.

**Cloak,** s. *bagalla*. *i.* px. (Am. *bacalla*). — an other kind: *batiya*. *n.* s. ox. plaited from bass.

**Clock,** s. *bilbila*. *i.* px. nat. sound.

**Clod,** s. *koditcha*. *i.* px. (*cotitcha*?) derived perhaps from *cota*, to plough. — 2. *tchafago*. *n.* ox. from *tchafaga*, to conglobe.

**Close,** adj. *caweza*. px. fem. *caweti*. ox. — 2. *gudz'i*. ox. probably from *guda*, to be full. — 3. *yabu*. ox. (Am. *yapu*). — to be -, *galma*. 1. px.

**Close,** v. a. *tchufa*. 1. px. — 2. espec. to - the mouth, *ukumza*. 1. px. and *ukumfad'a*. — to - by plaistering over with clay, *maraga*. 1. ppx. — to be -d, stopped, *ukama*. 1. ppx. and *tchucala*. 1. px. which also is used in the fig. sense, to be close, reserved; *ini dubiza tchucalad'a*, he is reserved in his words.

&lt;!-- PDF Page 301 (Part II Book Page 24) --&gt;
*(— 24 —)*

**Closeness,** s. *gudz'i*. *n.* ox. *midan kuni gudz'i caba*, this corn stands thick, lit. has closeness.

**Cloth,** s. *erbe*. *n.* ox.

**Clothes,** s. *waya*. *n.* ox. (Am. *woya*.)

**Cloud,** s. *dumeza*. *i.* px.

**Clover,** s. *zidiza*. nom. *zidifni*. px.

**Club,** s. *djirma*. *i.* px. — 2. *cala*. *n.* ox. — 3. *boku*. *n.* ox.

**Clutch,** s. *kote*. *n.* ox.

**Coagulated milk,** *ititu*. *n.* ox.

**Coal,** s. *kazala*. *i.* px.

**Coaxer,** s. *waz'awaz'tu*. *n.* ox.

**Cock,** s. *korma*. *i.* px. properly, the male of animals; often *handanco*, hen, is added: *korma handanco*.

**Cockered,** to be -, *ganania*. 1. ppx. *ilma ganani*, a cockered child.

**Coffee,** s. (the berry and the tree) *buna*. *i.* px. — when boiled: *gaʿwa*. (*caʿwa*) *n.* ox.

**Cogitate,** v. n. *heda*. 1. px. (Am. *hedaa*. 1. ppx.) — 2. *yado kuza*. 2. px. III. f. of *kua*, lit. to gather thoughts.

**Coin,** s. *zogita*. *i.* px. prop. salt. see money.

**Colander,** s. *gondji*. *n.* ox.

**Cold,** s. *d'amod'z'a*. *i.* px. *d'amod'z'i na caba*, lit. cold holds me, i. e. I feel cold.

**Cold,** to be -, *d'amod'a*. 1. ppx. *ani guda d'amod'era*, I feel very cold. — 2. *dilalã*. (*awa*) 2.

**Coldness,** s. *d'amod'z'a*. *i.* px.

**Colic,** s. *garanko waliti cabad'e*, I have a colic, lit. my belly holds itself together.

**Collar-sticks,** s. (for an ox to draw the plough) *gindjirti*. *n.* ox.

**Collect,** v. a. *funana*. 1. px. — 2. *kuza*. 2. px. III. f. of a root *kua* not in use.

**Colour,** s. *bifa*. *ti.* px.

**Comb,** s. *faqe*. *n.* ox. — 2. *fila*. *n.* ox. made of horn.

**Comb,** v. a. *fila*. 1. px. and II. f. *filad'a*. 1. ppx.

**Combat,** s. *lola*. *n.* ox. — 2. *fatche*. *n.* ox.

**Combat,** v. n. *dula*. 1. px. usually, *dulan dula* or *gadu dula*. — 2. *lola*. 1. px. — 3. *fatchiza*. 2. ppx. the root *fatcha* has not been found.

**Combatant,** s. *dula*. *i.* px.

&lt;!-- PDF Page 302 (Part II Book Page 25) --&gt;
*(— 25 —)*

**Come,** v. n. *d'ufa*. 1. px. and the Intens. *dud'ufa*. 1. px. — Imperat. mood, come! *kod'u*. px. plur. *kod'a*. px. Intens. *kokod'a*. ppx. — come on! *benu*. defective verb, px. — to - back, *debia*. 1. ppx. — to - down, *bua*. 1. px. — to - forward, *figa*. 1. px. — 2. *hica*. 1. px. — 3. *ziga*. 1. px. (Am. *çiga*.) — to - in, (as profit) *gȧ*. (*-aya*) 3. — 2. *bȧ*. (*-aa*) 1. — to - in a great mass, *yȧ*. (*-aa*) 1. — not to -, (fail in duty) *dad'aba*. 1. ppx. Intens. of *d'aba*, to miss etc. — to - to a place, *gȧ*. (*-aya*) 3. — to - up, to - to light, *bafad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *bȧ*, to go out. *yomu izan tulu dimtun matazani bafad'ani lafon d'enzite*, lit. when they made come up their heads over the red mountain, the infantry fled. — 2. *gubad'a*. 1. ppx. from *guba*, above. *ho ati tulu gubad'te*, when thou comest up the hill.

**Comfort,** v. a. *obziza*. 2. ppx. V. f. of a root *oba* not in use. — 2. *zozoba*. 1. ppx. but in the sense: to appease somebody by invented consolatory arguments, from *zoba*, to lie. — to be -ed, *obza*. 1. px.

**Comfortable,** to be -, *zarma*. 1. px. see, satisfy.

**Command,** s. *labzi*. *n.* ox. (Am.)

**Commander-in-chief,** s. *bai*. *n.* ox. from *bȧ*, to go out.

**Commence,** v. a. *caba*. 1. px. espec. *dyal-caba*, lit. to seize beneath. — to -, v. n. *kȧ*. (*-aa*) 1. constr. with the postp. *ti*. *ini iyuti kae*, lit. he rose to the crying, i. e. he began to cry.

**Commencement,** s. *mata*. *n.* ox. *ini mata dubi gade*, lit. he lost the head, i. e. the beginning, commencement of his speech.

**Commend,** v. a. *farza*. 1. px.

**Compact,** adj. *gudz'i*. ox. probably from *guda*, to be tall. — to be -, *galma*. 1. px.

**Companion,** s. *milto*. *n.* ox.

**Compare,** v. a. *akeka*. 1. px. *nu waliti akekna*, we compare each other. — 2. *qit'eza* and with *wal*, *wal qit'eza*. 2. px. III. f. of *qit'ã*, to be level, horizontal, equal, etc.

**Compassionate,** adj. *ardya*. px. — to be -, *ardya*. 1. px. — to become -, *ardyoma*. 1. px. passive of the foregoing.

**Complete,** adj. *qit'e*. ox. — to be -, *qit'ã*. (*-awa*) 2. — 2. *gumfura*. 1. ppx. probably a compound with *guma*, round. — to make -, *qit'eza*. 2. px. III. f. of *qit'ã*.

**Compliment,** v. a. *gabara*. 1. ppx. and Intens. *gagabara*. 1. ppx.

**Compose,** v. a. *dabalad'a*. 1. px. II. f. of *dabala*, to add, join. — to - a dispute, *dubi gadyêlfad'a*. 1. ppx. lit. to direct the words.

**Compound,** v. a. *dabalad'a*. 1. px.

**Comprehend,** v. a. fig. *caba*. 1. px. and II. f. *cabad'a*. 1. ppx. — to cause to -, *cabziza*. 2. px. III. f. and *cabzifad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *caba*.

**Conceal,** v. a. *d'okza*. 1. px. III. f. of a supposed root *d'oka*. — 2. *tchucala*. 1. px. and II. f. *tchucalad'a*. 1. ppx. — fig. *garati cabad'a*. 1. ppx. lit. to hold with the heart. — to be -ed, *tchucala*. 1. px. — 2. *d'okod'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. of a root *d'oka* not in use. — 3. *ukamfad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *ukama*, to shut up.

**Conceive,** v. a. *beka*. 1. px.

**Concern,** s. *makari*. *n.* ox.

**Concubine,** s. *zadjeda*. *n.* ox. This name is given to those girls whom the king takes when and how he likes out of their families and keeps them as assistants (*birmad'u*) of his first wife; their children have no right to the succession.

**Condemn,** v. a. *d'ad'a*. 1. px. infin. *d'ad'z'u*.

**Condescend,** v. n. *gaddebia*. 1. px. compounded of *gadi*, down and *debia* to return.

**Conduct,** v. a. *geza* 1. px. III. f. of *gȧ*, to approach. Passive *gefama*. 1. ppx. *ho Wacayo zi geza ingefamta*, if God lead thee thou art (well) led. — to - to a place, *galtsha*. 1. px. III. f. of *gala*, to go home.

**Conductor,** s. *milto*. *n.* ox.

**Confide,** v. n. *d'acad'a*. 1. ppx. *hind'acad'in*, do not confide.

**Confident,** to be -, *obza*. 1. px. III. f. of a root not yet found, probably *oba*.

**Confound,** v. a. *didira*. 1. px. *dubinza didirame*, lit. his words were changed, i. e. he became confused.

**Confused,** to be -, *muzã*. (*-awa*) 2.

**Conglobe,** v. a. *tchafaga*. 1. ppx.

**Congratulation,** s. *êba*. *n.* ox.

**Conjurer,** s. *djila*. *i.* px. see magician. — 2. *radji*. *n.* ox. (Am.)

**Connected,** to be - with, *matana*. 1. ppx.

**Conquer,** v. a. *moa*. 1. px. — 2. *bodja*. 1. px. — to have -ed, *bodjeziza*. 2. px. V. f. of *bodja*.

**Consanguineous,** adj. *akaku*. *n.* ox. (also used as a subst.) — 2. *como*. *n.* ox.

**Consanguinity,** s. *akaku*. *n.* ox. collect.

**Considerable,** adj. *ulfa*. px. lit. heavy. — to be -, *ulfã*. (*-awa*) 2.

**Console,** v. a. see, comfort.

**Consumption,** s. *t'it'a*. *n.* ox.

**Contempt,** v. a. *zalbiza*. 2. ppx. III. f. of *zalba*, to be light, slight.

**Content,** to be -, *zarma*. 1. px. *garanko zarmudida*, my heart is discontented.

**Continually,** adv. *ama-ama*, or *amama*. px.

**Convey,** v. a. *fida*. 1. px. see, bring.

**Cool,** v. a. (by blowing) *bubiza*. 2. ppx. from *bube*, wind. — 2. *hafarza*. 1. px. probably related to *hafura*, breath. — to - one's self, *dilalã*. (*-awa*) 2. — 2. *cabanã*. (*-awa*) 2.

**Cool,** to be or become -, *cabanã*. (*-awa*) 2.

**Copious,** adj. *dilbi*. ox.

**Copulate,** v. n. (of sexes) *cota*. 1. px. properly to bore, dig.

**Cord,** s. *malat'i*. *n.* ox. — 2. *wadaro*. *n.* ox. — a cord, bound to the neck and to the right hind foot of the horse, to prevent his running away: *dagala*. *i.* px. (Am. *takala*, Akaf. *dacala*.)

**Corn,** s. in general, *mid'an*. (*mid'ani*) inv. ox. — - in the ear, a single, *guma*. *n.* ox. — 2. *tchala*. *n.* ox. — a sort of -, the Durrha of the Arabians, *mizinga*. There are many species of it: *mizinga guradja* (black), *miz. dima* (red), *miz. adi* (white), *miz. handjiro* - *goe* - *dapo* - *t'uçe* etc. — roasted -, a very common food, *akami*. *n.* ox.

**Cornice,** s. (around the room, on which utensils are kept) *harati*. *s.* ox. (Am. *arati*.)

**Corpulent,** adj. *cabad'u*. ox. from *caba*, to have, take. — to be -, *midaga*. 1. ppx.

**Correct,** v. a. *ad'aba*. 1. ppx. probably from *d'aba*, to put. — 2. *d'ana*. 1. px. usually with *yaka*, punishment. *Wacayo yaka zi d'anufdyira*, God will punish thee. — 3. *gadyêlza*. 1. px. III. f. of *gadyêla*, to be right. — 4. *zerad'a*. 1. ppx.

**Correction,** s. *t'uri*. *n.* ox. — 2. *zera*. *n.* ox.

**Corrupt,** v. a. *balʿeza*. 1. ppx. — v. n. *tortora*. 1. ppx. — 2. *zamã*. (*-awa*), espec. of eatables.

**Cost,** v. n. to - much, *miʿã*. (*-awa*) 2. lit. to be sweet. *barrana rooni inimiʿofti*, this year goats are sweet, i. e. in demand, of great value, dear.

**Costive,** adj. *hud'o*. ox.

**Cotton,** s. *dyerbi*. *n.* ox.

**Couch,** s. *afa*. *ni.* px. — to prepare a -, *afa afa*. 1. px. *afa na afi*, prepare me a bed.

**Cough,** s. *ut'ala*. *n.* ox. or *ut'alo*. *n.* ox. from *ut'ala*, to leap, jump. — 2. espec. of phthisical persons, *cake*. *n.* ox.

**Cough,** v. n. *cufȧ*. (*-aa*) 1. — to -, (in order to spit out), *akfad'a*. 1. ppx. nat. sound.

**Counsel,** s. *mala*. *n.* ox.

**Counsel,** v. a. *mala*. 1. px. *ufi male*, lit. he has counselled himself, i. e. he acted independently.

**Count,** v. a. *doa*. 1. px. and the II. f. *doad'a*. 1. ppx. *lonke hindoad'in*, do not count thy cattle, (scl. because it is an unlucky omen.) — 2. *lakã*. (*-awa*) 2.

**Countenance,** s. *fula*. *i.* px.

**Country,** s. *bia*. nom. *biti*. px. — a - which is cold and uninhabitable on account of great forests, *bada*. *n.* ox. the opposite of *gamodji*.

**Countryman,** s. *cafi*. *n.* ox. (Ag.) — 2. *abali*. *n.* ox. spec. f. *abalitcha*. *i.* which is also used as an abusive word. — 3. *mado*. *n.* ox. (Am.)

**Courageous,** adj. *god'a*. px. — 2. *djagna*. px. — to be -, *djagna*. 1. px. — 2. *obza*. 1. px.

**Course,** s. *kad'u*. *n.* ox.

**Courser,** s. *djolle*. *n.* ox.

**Cousin,** s. *durbi*. *n.* ox.

**Cover,** s. *cundje*. *n.* ox. — 2. *cadad'o*. *n.* ox.

**Covetous,** to be -, *zazȧ*. (*-aa*) 1. see avaricious.

**Cow,** s. *zã*. (contr. from *zawa*; — Aga knows only this uncontracted form) nom. *zani*. Akafed'e used it as masc. — a - two years old, *rad'a*. *i.* px. (Am. *rada*. nom. *rani*). — the - which is slaughtered at nuptials, *rako*. *n.* ox. — - big with calf, *zilga*. *i.* px. — milch-cow, *zilga*. *i.* px.

**Cow-pox,** s. *finno*. (Ak. *finnʿo*.) *n.* ox. see, vaccine.

**Coward,** adj. *lucna*. (Ak. *lugna*.) px.

**Crack,** v. n. *cacak-djed'a*, lit. to say „*cacak*“, nat. sound. — 2. *cacakza*. 1. px. — 3. *cawʿiza*. 2. ppx. from the nat. sound „*caw*“. — to - with the whip, *catchiza*. 2. ppx. v. denom. from *catchi*, whip. — 2. *tchacad'a*. 1. ppx. nat. sound.

**Crackle,** v. n. (of fire) *cawʿiza*. 2. ppx. from the nat. sound „*caw*“.

**Craftsman,** s. *tumtu*. *n.* ox. fem. *tumtiti*. *n.* ox. which is also used as an abusive word, because an artificers life appears effeminate to the warlike Galla.

**Crafty,** adj. *hate*. ox. (Am.)

**Crape,** to - off, v. a. *folocza*. 1. ppx. III. f. of *foloca*, to suppurate. — 2. *zolocza*. 1. ppx. v. denom. from *zoloqe*, crust of bread.

**Crash,** v. n. *cab-djed'a*. 1. nat. sound, lit. to say „*cab*“. — 2. *tchaba*. 1. px. — to -, v. a. *docoza*. 2. px. — 2. *hurreza*. 1. px. III. f. of *hurrã*. to burst. — 3. *tchabza*. 1. px. III. f. of *tchaba*, and IV. f. *tchabzad'a*. 1. ppx. in the recipr. sense.

**Crash,** s. *indiditsha*. *i.* px.

**Crawl,** v. n. *loa*. 1. px. — to make -, *loza*. 2. px. III. f.

**Create,** v. a. *uma*. 1. px. (only said of God). — created, adj. *uma*. px.

**Creep,** v. n. *loa*. 1. px. — to - on all fours, *daima*. 1. px. — to - out of the egg, *borukza*. 1. px. III. f. of *boruka*, to break. — to - through a thing where it is necessary to bow in order to

avoid running against a wall or any other object, *huluga*. 1. ppx. v. denom. from *hula*, thorough-fare.

**Crippled,** adj. *goz'u*. ox, see mutilated. — to walk lame, *goñoñã*. 2. px.

**Cross,** v. a. (a hill) *caria*. 1. ppx. — to - one's self with blood (during the sacrifice), *d'ica cabad'a*, lit. to take the blood (of the victim) for one's self.

**Crowd,** s. *tila*. *i.* px.

**Crown,** s. (of the head) *banti*. *n.* ox. (Ak. *bandi*.)

**Crumble,** v. a. *docoza*. 2. px. — to - (bread) into, *fadafad'a*. 1. ppx. — to -, v. n. *gororia*. 1. ppx.

**Crumpled,** to become -, *curifama*. 1. ppx. the passive of *curia*, a root not in use; *gogan kuni curifamera*, this skin is shrivelled. — 2. *t'olaga*. 1. ppx.

**Crupper,** s. *udela*. *i.* px. (Am. *hudela*.)

**Crush,** v. a. *borukza*. 1. px. III. f. of *boruka* to break. — 2. *t'uca*. 1. px. (Am. *tuca*.) — 3. *d'id'a*. 1. px. (Am. *dita*.) — to - by treading on, *matcharakza*. 1. px.

**Crust,** s. *foloqe*. *n.* ox. — 2. *zoloqe*. *n.* ox.

**Cry,** v. n. *iya*. 1. px. — 2. (from pain) *gadoda*. 1. px. — 3. *tcharana*. 1. ppx. this word is espec. used of the battle-cry; *duli tcharane*, the soldiers raised the battle-cry. — to - like a hen which has laid an egg, *kakiza*. 2. ppx. nat. sound.

**Cudgel,** s. *furguge*. *n.* ox. — 2. *cala*. *n.* ox.

**Cue,** s. *gonogona*. *n.* ox. from *gonogona*, to plait.

**Dress,** v. a. (said of women) *olgad'a*. 1. px. from *ola*. — 2. *uwiza*. 2. ppx. constr. with the postp. *ti*. *ani ziti uwiza*, I dress thee. — to - one's self, *uffad'a*. 1. ppx. both these verbs seem to be formed from the reflect. pron. *ufi* (*ofi*, *of*.) — to - the hair, *fila*. 1. px. and II. f. *filad'a*. 1. ppx. — 2. *mata d'ã*, lit. to beat the head. — to - raw hides, *lucã*. (*awa*) 2. — to - away, *adema*. 1. px. — 2. *darba*. 1. px. 2d pers. *dabarta*. with transposed consonants, see Gram. num. 131. *darbi!* give way, go away! *dabarza*. 1. px. (*-aa*) 1. but always in the sense: on the same way on which one came. — to - clandestinely, surreptiously, *zoka*. 1. px. (Ag.) — to - down, *gaddebia*. 1. ppx. comp. of *gadi*, down and *debia*, to return. — to - frequently to a person, to - to see, *marmara*. 1. ppx. — to - forward, *ziga*. 1. px. (Am.) — to - home, *gala*. 1. px. and II. f. *galad'a*. 1. ppx. — to - in, into, *namʿa*. 1. px. — to - to pieces, *çarmama*. 1. px. — to - off, *bȧ*. (contr. from *baa*) 1. *gogon kuni ira baudida*, this skin will not go off. — 2. *darba*. 1. px. — to - on horse back, *farta yabod'a*. 1. ppx. — to - out, *bȧ*. (*-aa*) 1. — 2. *bobȧ*. (*-aa*) 1. both in the sense of: to take a walk. — 3. (in the morning) *ganamfad'a*. 1. ppx. from *ganama*, morning. — to -

round, *marza*. 1. px. III f. of *mara*, to turn. — to - round a thing, *nanã*. (*-awa*) 2. — to - secretly, lightly, *gadiza*. 2. ppx. (Am. *cat'iza*. 2. ppx.) III. f. of *gada*, to go a robbing or upon a warlike adventure at night. — to - to a place, *gala*. 1. px. and II. f. *galad'a*. 1. ppx. — 2. *bá*. (*-aa*) 1. constr. with *ti*. *tulu Meeçati bae*, he went upon the mountain *Meeça*. — to - to meet, (either in a friendly or hostile intent) *zima*. 1. px. — to - towards an object, *gadyêla*. 1. px. — to - through, *tchea*. 1. px. — let us -, *benu*. px. verb defect. probably the corrupted 1. pers. plur. pres. of *bá*, to go out.

**Good,** s. *tola*. *n.* ox.

**Good,** adj. *gari*. ox. *ini nati garid'a*, lit. he is good to me, i. e. he loves me. *aka gariti*, as good as possible. — to be -, *arara*. 1. px. — 2. *lafa*. 1. px. — 3. *tola*. 1. px. — to become -, *garoma*. 1. px. from *gari*. — to make -, *ararza*. 1. px. III. f. of *arara*. — *toltcha*. 1. px. III. f. of *tola*. — to make - for one's self, *tolfad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *tola*. — to do -, *zifa*. 1. px. *zifa!* used as a proverbial wish: may it prosper! — 2. *tola*. 1. px. *areri kuni naf toludide*, this medicine was not wholesome to me.

**Goodness,** s. *arara*. *i.* px. — 2. *marara*. *n.* ox. — 3. *tola*. *n.* ox.

**Goose,** s. *dakie*. *n.* ox.

**Gore,** v. a. *ȧ* (contr. from *aa*) 1. *koromin wal ate*, bulls gore.

**Gourd,** s. *buqe*. *n.* ox. — the marrow of gourds or gourd-like fruits, together with the kernels, *midjiran*. inv. ox.

**Gout,** s. *d'anʿo*. *n.* ox. (Am. and Aga: *dano*.)

**Governor,** s. *moti*. *n.* ox. — 2. (of a town or a village) *aba ganda*, lit. father of the town.

**Grace,** s. *arara*. *i.* px. — 2. *marara*. *n.* ox. — 3. *t'uri*. *n.* ox. which see in part first.

**Graceful,** to be -, *nȧ*. (contr. from *naa*) 1.

**Gracious,** to be -, *tola*. 1. px.

**Grain,** s. (in general) *mid'an*. (*mid'ani*) inv. ox. — (in the ear) *guma*. *n.* ox. — a single - of an ear, *tchala*. *n.* ox.

**Grand-father,** s. *akakayu*. *n.* px.

**Grand-mother,** s. *akiyo*. inv. px. (Am. *akao*. px.)

**Grant,** v. a. *nȧ*. (contr. from *naa*) 1.

**Grass,** s. *marga*. *i.* px. — high -, *dagala*. *n.* ox. — a certain sort of -, *lenza*. *n.* ox. see part I. — another one, *tshogorza*. *i.* px.

**Grate,** v. a. *cora*. 1. px. — *hagoga*. 1. ppx. — 3. *hoca*. 1. px. and II. f. *hocad'a*. 1. ppx. (Am.) — 4. *zukuma*. 1. ppx.

**Grave,** s. *bola*. *i.* px. — 2. *awala*. *i.* px. — *awaldigeza*. *i.* px. a certain animal which makes its den in the earth like a fox and is a despoiler of graves like the hyena; lit. grave-spoiler, perhaps the *Mokel* of the Yumale.

**Gravel,** s. (in the bed of a river) *tchiradja*. *i.* px.

**Graver,** s. (instrum.) *horta*. *n.* ox.

**Gray-headed,** to be -, *cora*. 1. px.

**Graze,** v. n. *mura*. 1. px.

**Grease,** s. *baeza*. *n.* ox. — 2. *mora*. *i.* ox.

**Grease,** v. a. *diba*. 1. px. — 2. *muda*. 1. px. — to - one's self, *dibad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. of *diba*. — 2. *mudad'a*. II. f. of *muda*.

**Great,** adj. *guda*. ox. — very -, *gudidja*. px. — to be -, *guda*. 1. px. — to make -, *gudiza*. 2. px.

**Greater,** to be -, *tchala*. 1. px. a singular verb which contains the comparative degree. — to make -, *tchaliza*. 2. ppx. III. f. of *tchala*. — to get made -, *tchalziza*. V. f. of *tchala*.

**Great-gut,** (Intestinum rectum) s. *garatsha*. *i.* px. — (Colon) *zamaga*. *ni.* ppx.

**Green,** adj. *t'ilo*. ox. — to be -, *dyid'a*. 1. px. — 2. *had'ã*. (*-awa*) 2. — 3. *magalã*. (*-awa*) 2. (Am.) — 4. *tchurca*. 1. px. esp. in the sense of: to be unripe. (Am. *tchôrca*.) — 5. *t'ilã*. (*-awa*) 2.

**Greet,** v. a. *naga gafad'a*. 1. ppx. lit. to ask after one's peace. — 2. *naga-naga djed'a*, lit. to say: peace, peace! Several forms of greeting, see part I. under *ola*, *bula*, *faya* etc.

**Gridiron,** s. *codi*. *n.* ox. — 2. *duba*. *n.* ox.

**Grief,** s. *gaddayi*. *n.* px.

**Grieve,** v. a. *warerza*. 1. px. III. f. of *warera*, to be afraid. — to -, v. n. *gadda*. 1. px.

**Groan,** v. n. *ada*. 1. px. espec. of women in labour. — 2. *t'it'a*. 1. px.

**Groom,** s. *nao*. *n.* ox. a servant who rides behind the king and leads his *farta lola* (battle-steed), or *djole* (riding-horse).

**Grow,** v. n. *d'alad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. of *d'ala*, to give birth to. — 2. *marga*. 1. px. — to cause to -, *magarza*. 1. px. III. f. of *marga*. — to - up, *dyabad'a*. 1. ppx. *ani dyabanan*, when I was of full growth.

**Growl,** s. *barodz'u*. *n.* ox.

**Growl,** v. n. *korriza*. 2. ppx.

**Grudgeon,** s. *hincatchi*. *n.* ox.

**Grumble,** v. n. *korriza*. 2. ppx.

**Guard,** v. a. *barara*. 1. px. — 2. *ega*. 1. px. — 3. *gombiza*. 3. ppx. III. f. of *gomfa*, to cover the head. — 4. *tikza*. 1. px. III. f. of *tika*, to be a shepherd. — to - one's self, *gombiza*. — 2. *d'ad'a*. 1. px.

**Guardian-angel,** s. *ayana*. *i.* px.

**Guest,** s. *dulbe*. *n.* ox. — 2. *kezuma*. *ni.* ppx.

**Guide,** s. *bai*. *n.* ox. — 2. *mata*. *n.* ox. — 3. *momoti*. *n.* ox.

**Guilt,** s. *hama*. *n.* ox. and Intens. *hamtu*. *n.* ox. *ani wa hamtu izaraṅ argu*, lit. I see no guilt in him, I find him not guilty. — 2. *tshuba*. *n.* ox. see, sin.

**Guilty,** adj. *hama*. ox. — to be -, *gafa*. 1. px. impers. *nutu gafe*, we are guilty. — 2. *hama*. 1. px. and II. f. *hamad'a*. 1. ppx. — 3. *tshubama*. 1. ppx. passive of *tshuba*, to plunge. *hintshubamin*, do not sin.

**Guinea-hen,** s. *zololia*. *n.* ox.

**Gulp down,** v. a. *ligimza*. 1. px. III. f. of an unfound root *ligima*.

**Gums,** s. *ao*. *n.* ox. (Am.)

**Health,** s. *faya*. *n.* ox. *ani fayaṅ cabu*, lit. I have no health; or *ani faya d'aba*, I find no health, i. e. I am sick. — 2. *naga*. *n.* ox.

**Healthy,** adj. *faya*. ox. — to be or become -, *faya*. 1. px. — to make -, *fayiza*. 2. ppx. III. f. of *faya*.

**Heed,** v. a. *ega*. 1. px. prop. to wait.

(intr.) Intens. of an unused root *gara*. — to - somebody out of difficulties, *hulugza*. 1. ppx. and espec. *hulugziza*. 2. px. III. and V. f. of *huluga*, to creep through.

**Her,** possess. pron. *z'i*. affix. (Am. *z'e*.) *hidjolenz'i*, her children.

**Herald,** s. *labzi*. *n.* ox. — 2. *erga*. *n.* ox. from *erga*, to send.

**Hog,** s. *boye*. *n.* ox.

## I.

**I,** pron. *ani*. px. Intens. *anatu*, *anatᵃ*.

(*halkani*) inv. ox. — a certain part of the -, *wari*. *n.* ox. — to pass the -, *bula*. 1. px. — good -! *nagan* (*naguman*, *nagaiti*) *buli*, lit. pass the night in peace! — to go upon a warlike adventure at -, *gada*. 1. px. — -'s lodging, *irge dyizu*, lit. place to lie.

(It is divided as follows: $\frac{1}{2}$ *amole*: *tchabnana*. *i.* px. from *tchaba*, to break; — $\frac{1}{4}$ *amole*: *kurmana*. *n.* px. — $\frac{1}{8}$ *amole*: *gimaz'i*. *n.* ox. — $\frac{1}{12}$ *amole*: *gedele*. *n.* ox. (Ag. *gadale*. Am. *fotoqe*.) Two *amole* make a *mogor*. (*mogora*)

**Sun,** s. *adu*. *n.* ox. which is the destroying principle whilst *biftu*. *n.* ox. marks the enlivening one. — sun-shine, *biftu*. *n.* ox.

**Supper,** s. *hirbata*. *n.* ox. — to take the -, *hirbata ñau'a*.

**Support,** s. *bad'u*. *n.* ox. — 2. *borati*. *n.* ox. — 3. *cofni*. *n.* px. — 4. *irko*. *n.* ox. — 5. *d'ai*. *n.* ox. *Wacayo d'aikoti*, God is my support.

**Suppurate,** v. n. *foloca*. 1. ppx.

**Sure,** adj. *d'uga*. ox. — 2. *hunde*. ox.

**Surety,** *luka*. *ni.* ox.? see part I.

**Surround,** v. a. *mara*. 1. px. constr. with *ira*.

**Survey,** v. a. *doa*. 1. px.

**Suspend,** v. a. to - a thing so that it does not touch another object, *raraza*. 2. px. III. f. of *rará*, to hang. — 2. to - a thing in such a manner that it leans against the wall, *faniza*. 2. ppx. — to be -ed, *rará*. (*-aa*) 1.

**Suture,** s. *harira*. *i.* px.

**Swallow,** s. (Ornith.) *dalle*. *n.* ox.

**Swallow,** s. (throat) *ligimzitu*. *n.* ox. (Am. *ligimza*. *n.* ox.)

**Swallow,** v. a. *ligimza*. 1. px. III. f. of an unfound root *ligima*.

**Swarm,** v. n. *walawala*. 1. ppx. — 2. *wazawaza*. 1. ppx.

**Swear,** v. n. *kakad'a*. 1. ppx.

**Sweat,** s. *dafca*. *n.* ox.

**Sweat,** v. n. *dafca*. 1. px. — 2. *finina*. 1. px.

**Sweep,** v. a. *haga*. 1. px.

**Sweet,** adj. *inimida*. px. — to be -, *miʿa*. (*-awa*) 2. — to become -, *tiltilã*. (*-awa*) 2.

**Sweet-heart,** s. *dyalala*. *i.* px.

**Sweetness,** s. *komdo*. *n.* ox.

**Swell,** v. n. *bokoka*. 1. ppx. — 2. *dorroba*. 1. ppx. — 3. *itã*. (*-awa*) 2. — 4. *yȧ*. (contr. from *yaa*) 1. — to make -, *bokokza*. 1. px. III. f. of *bokoka*.

**Swim,** v. n. *daka*. 1. px. or *biz'an daka*. (*biz'an*, water.)

**Swine,** s. *boye*. *n.* ox.

**Swollen,** adj. *bokolu*. ox.

**Sword,** s. see, sabre. — sword-belt, *têba*. *n.* ox. prop. thong.

**Syphilis,** s. *fando*. *n.* ox. Prov. *ini fando afaniti caba*, lit. he has the - in the mouth, used to denote a person of a very bad character.

## T.

**Table,** s. *gadila*. *n.* ox. — a little - with one foot, *gabate*. *n.* ox. — a - made of fruits, *lemata*. *ni.* ox.

**Tail,** s. *dubo*. *n.* ox. — 2. *ege*. *n.* ox. *ini ege nurra caba*, lit. he has the - on us, i. e. he is close behind us. — the tip of the - of oxen or horses, *fatcho*. *n.* ox. from *fatchiza*, to fight, because these animals „fight“ with this bunch of hair against the flies.

**Tailor,** s. *hod'itu*. *n.* ox. from *hod'a*, to sew.

**Take,** v. a. *caba*. 1. px. and II. f. *cabad'a*. 1. ppx. (III. and IV. f. *cabziza*. 2. px. and *cabzifad'a*. 1. ppx. to cause to be taken). — 2. *fud'a*. 1. px. (II. f. *fud'ad'a*. 1. ppx. to - for one's self; III. f. *fuziza*, to cause to take). — 3. *gurrad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. of *gurra*, to carry away. — to - for, *cabad'a*. 1. ppx. *hatu na cabad'ani*, they took me for a foe. — 2. *djed'a*. 1. px. *nu izan dina djene*, we took them for foes. — 3. *zea*. 1. px. — to - off, *baza*. 2. px. III. f. of *bȧ*, to go out. — 2. *hartchad'a*. 1. px. II. f. of *hartchá*, to fall off. — to - up, *bafad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *bá*, to go out. — 2. *kaza*. 2. px. III. f. and *kafad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *kã*, to rise. (V. and VI. f. *kaziza*. 2. ppx. and *kazifad'a*. 1. ppx. to have taken up.) — to - by force of arms, *bodja*. 1. px. — to - about the head, *zogolfad'a*. 1. ppx. v. denom. from *zogola*, upper-garment. — to - care, *ilalad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. of *ilala*, to see. — to - an oath, *kakad'a*. 1. ppx.

**Tale,** s. *raga*. *n.* ox. — to relate -s, *raga*. 1. px.

**Tale-bearer,** s. *lafaftu*. *n.* ox. or *lafaftudatu*. *n.* ox.

**Talent,** s. *zura*. *i.* px.

**Talk,** s. *dubi*. *n.* ox.

**Talk,** v. a. *dubad'a*. 1. ppx.

**Talker,** s. *kaz'akaz'tu*. *n.* ox.

**Tall,** adj. *d'êra*. px. — 2. *guda*. ox. — 3. *lotchi*. ox. — to be -, *guda*. 1. px. — 2. *lotchã*. (*-awa*) 2.

**Tallow-candle,** s. *gipo mora* or *gibbo mora*. (*gipo*. *n.* ox. candle, *mora*. *i.* ox. fat.)

**Tambourine,** s. *dibe*. *n.* ox.

**Tame,** adj. *hori*. ox. — 2. (of plants without thorns and of beasts) *kaça*. px.

**Tap,** v. a. *dururfad'a*. 1. ppx. IV. f. of *durura*, to drop. — 2. *dyigza*. 1. px. III. f. of *dyiga*, to well.

**Tape-worm,** s. *duma*. *n.* ox. see part I. *het'o*.

**Tarry,** v. n. *hafa*. 1. px. — 2. *tura*. 1. px. see, stay.

**Taste,** v. a. *miʿã*. (*-awa*) 2. — 2. *una*. 1. px. — 3. *cama*. 1. px. (perhaps contracted from *cabna* and related to *caba*, to hold.) — 4. *ilala*. 1. px. prop. to see. — 5. *afanin caba*, lit. to take with the mouth. — to cause to taste, *afanin cabziza*. 2. px. or *cabzifad'a*. 1. ppx. — 2. *unziza*. 2. ppx. III. f. of *una*.

**Tattle,** v. n. *abakad'a*. 1. px. (Am. used it in the sense of: to gather alms.) — 2. *dudubad'a*. 1. ppx. Intens. of *dubad'a*, to speak.

**Teach,** v. a. *barziza*. 1. px. III. f. of *bara*, to learn. — 2. *bekziza*. 2. px. III. f. of *beka*, to know. — 3. *ledjiza*. 2. ppx.

**Teacher,** s. *azgari*. *n.* ox. — 2. *barziza*. *fni.* px.

**Tear,** s. *imiman*. inv. ox. — to bring to -s, *boziza*. 2. ppx. III. f. of *boa*, to weep.

**Tear,** v. a. *kuta*. 1. px. and II. f. *kutad'a*. 1. px. — 2. *buta*. 1. px. — 3. *tarzaza*. 2. px. III. f. of *tarzá*, to be ragged. — 4. *tchita*. 1. px. and III. f. *tchitiza*. 1. ppx.

**Teat,** s. *mutsha*. *n.* ox.

**Tedious,** to be -, *nufa*. 1. px.

**Tell,** v. a. *djed'a*. 1. px. — 2. *hima*. 1. px. and II. f. *himad'a*. 1. ppx.

**Ten,** *kudan*. ox. (perhaps related to *kuta*, to cut.)

**Tend,** v. a. *tikza*. 1. px. III. f. of *tika*, to be a shepherd. — 2. (nurse) *amad'a*. 1. ppx.

**Tender,** adj. *kitchu*. ox. — to be -, *lafa*. 1. px.

**Tendon,** s. *ribu*. *n.* ox.

**Tepid,** to be -, *buluga*. 1. ppx. — to make -, *bulugza*. 1. ppx.

**Term,** s. *caba*. *n.* ox. from *caba*, to hold. — 2. (jur.) *d'abte*. *n.* ox.

**Terminate,** v. a. *fit'a*. 1. px.

**Terrify,** v. a. *naziza*. 2. ppx. III. f. of *ná*, to be afraid. — 2. *rogomza*. 1. px. III. f. of *rogoma*, to tremble. — 3. *warerza*. 1. px. III. f. of *warera*, to be afraid.

**Testicle,** s. *zeru*. *n.* ox.

**Tetter,** s. *ari*. *n.* ox. prop. hoarfrost.

**Thanks,** s. *galata*. *ni.* px. — 2. *hocuban*. inv. ox.

**Thank,** v. n. *êbiza*. 2. ppx. III. f. of an unused root *éba*. — 2. *galata bafad'a*. 1. ppx. lit. to make go out the thanks.

**That,** pron. *zana*. px. affix. nom. *zuni*.

**That,** conj. *aka*. px. constr. with the mood *ti*. *aka ati na duba ademtuti zi god'a*, I shall make that thou walk behind me. — 2. *kan*. prop. the abbrev. demonstr. pron. *kana*. — 3. *oto*. px. (Am. and Ag. *utu*). *ani fed'a oto nu atchi dyirana*, I would (that) we were there.

**Thatcher,** s. *adyertu*. *n.* ox. from *adyera*, to make a roof upon a house.

**Theft,** s. *malato*. *n.* ox.

**Their,** poss. pron. 3rd pers. plur. *zani*. px. affix.

**Then,** adv. temp. *djenan*. px. prop. the participle preter. of *djed'a*, to say. (Am.) — 2. *gafa zana*, lit. at that time, on that day. — 3. *gulana*. px. — 4. *hamanuman*. px. (Am. *hamanani*. px. Ag. *açuman* or *açumanimo*. ox.) — 5. *hoana*. px. lit. when this. — as interjection, *ka*, affix. for ex. *ilalika!* look then, pray.

**Thence,** adv. *atchiyi*. px.

**There,** adv. *atchi*. ox. — there and there, *gara-gara* or *gargar*.

**Therefore,** adv. *kan*, (the abbreviated *kana*, this) followed by the postp. *fu*. — 2. a compound of *kana* and *fu*: *kanafu*. (*kanaf*, *kanafan*, *kanumaf*, *kanumafan*) px. — 3. *kanarati* or *kanumarati*. px. (*kana - ira - ti*.)

**Thick,** adj. *bokolu*. ox. — 2. *cabad'u*. ox. — 3. *furda*. ox. — 4. *yabu*. ox. (Am. *yapu*.) — to be -, *furda*. 1. px. (*furdã*?) — to become -, *furdad'a*. 1. px. — 2. *gudêldjã*. (*-awa*) 2. — 3. *itã* (*-awa*) 2.

**Thicket,** s. *bozona*. *i.* px.

**Thickness,** s. *tshoma*. *n.* ox. *godiyon kuni tshoma caba*, this ox is fat, lit. has thickness.

**Thief,** s. *hatu*. *n.* ox. as a collective word fem. — spec. f. *hatitsha*. *i.* px. also used as a low-word.

**Thigh,** s. *gudeda*. *ni.* px. — 2. *luka*. *n.* ox. (Ak. *luga*.)

**Thin,** adj. *baqe*. ox. — 2. *habbi*. ox. (Am. *hapi*.) — 3. (of fluids) *cacalʿi*. ox. (Am. *cacalʿa*.) — to be -, (of fluids) *calʿa*. 1. px. — to become -, *hugada*. 1. ppx.

**Thine,** pron. adj. *wanke*. px. see part I. *wa*.

**Thing,** s. (in general) *hori*. *n.* ox. — 2. *god'a*. *n.* ox. particularly utensils.

**Think,** v. n. *cabad'a*. 1. ppx. II. f. of *caba*, to take. — 2. *heda*. 1. px. (Am. *hedaa*. 1. ppx.) — 3. *yada*. 1. px. — 4. *zea*. 1. px.

**Thirst,** s. *d'êbu*. *n.* ox. — to quench the -, *d'êbu baza*, lit. to make go out the thirst.

**To,** prep. *gara*. prep. px., prop. a subst. „part“. Instead of the personal pronoun, *gara* takes always the possessive; f. e. *garako*, *garake*, *garaz'i* etc. to me, to thee, to her, etc. — 2. *irge*. ox. lit. place. — 3. *ti*. postp. affix. — to-day, adv. *harʿa*. px. **Wager,** s. *cote*. *n.* ox. from *cota*, to bore, because when men wish to lay a wager, they hook the little fingers together by „boring“ in each others' hands.

## Y.

**Yard,** s. *d'und'uma*. *ni.* ppx. — a half *d'und'uma* is: *djigele*. *n.* ox. prop. elbow.

**Yawn,** v. n. *hamumad'a*. 1. ppx. nat. sound.

**Year,** s. *barra*. *i.* px. the different compounds with *barra* see in part I. — Aman said *woga*. (*woca*) *n.* ox. perhaps the same as *waca*, heaven, God.

**Yelk,** s. *ot'a*. *n.* ox. from *ot'ã*, to be yellow.

**Yellow,** adj. *dalatcha*. px. — 2. *ot'a*. ox. — to be -, *dalatchã*. (*-awa*) 2. — 2. *ot'ã*. (*-awa*) 2. — to make -, *dalatchiza*. 2. px. III. f. of *dalatchã*.

**Yes,** adv. *e* or *ee*. px.

**Yesterday,** adv. *kaleza*. px. — exactly -, *kalezuma*. ppx. — the day before -, *d'engata*. (*d'engad'a*) ppx.

**Yoke,** s. (for cattle) *gambari*. *n.* ox.

**Yon, yonder,** pron. *zana*. px. nom. *zuni*. px.

**You,** pron. *izin*. (*izini*) ox. Intens. *izintu*. px. — to you, *iziniti*. px.

**Young,** adj. *d'alad'u*. ox. — - of beasts, *d'ala*. *n.* ox. and *d'alo*. *n.* ox. — 2. *ilma*. *i.* px. fem. *ilmo*. *n.* ox. plur. *ilman*. ox. — a - man, *dargago*. *n.* ox. — 2. *dargageza*. *i.* px. — a - man before twenty five years, *qero*. *n.* ox.

**Youth,** s. *dargago*. *n.* ox.; as a collect. fem. — 2. *dargageza*. *i.* px. see the foregoing word.
